            "============================================================\n",
            "ONE-HOT ENCODING vs WORD2VEC\n",
            "============================================================\n",
            "\n",
            "ONE-HOT ENCODING\n",
            "----------------\n",
            "- Each word gets a vector.\n",
            "- Vector contains mostly 0s and one 1.\n",
            "- Does not learn semantic meaning.\n",
            "- Similar words are not automatically represented as similar.\n",
            "- Vector size depends on vocabulary size.\n",
            "\n",
            "\n",
            "WORD2VEC\n",
            "--------\n",
            "- Learns word representations from surrounding words.\n",
            "- Each word gets a dense numerical vector.\n",
            "- Can capture relationships between words.\n",
            "- Similar words can have similar vectors.\n",
            "- Vector size is chosen using vector_size.\n",
            "- Uses surrounding/context words to learn representations.\n",
            "\n"
          ]
        }
      ],
      "source": [
        "from sklearn.preprocessing import OneHotEncoder\n",
        "words = [\n",
        "    ['cat'],\n",
        "    ['dog'],\n",
        "    ['apple'],\n",
        "    ['car']\n",
        "]\n",
        "encoder = OneHotEncoder(sparse_output=False)\n",
        "result = encoder.fit_transform(words)\n",
        "print(\"Words:\", encoder.categories_[0])\n",
        "print(\"\\nOne-Hot Encodings:\")\n",
        "print(result)\n",
        "!pip install -q gensim\n",
        "from gensim.models import Word2Vec\n",
        "sentences = [\n",
        "    [\"python\", \"is\", \"a\", \"language\"],\n",
        "    [\"java\", \"is\", \"a\", \"language\"],\n",
        "    [\"python\", \"is\", \"easy\"],\n",
        "    [\"java\", \"is\", \"easy\"],\n",
        "    [\"python\", \"is\", \"powerful\"],\n",
        "    [\"java\", \"is\", \"powerful\"]\n",
        "]\n",
        "model = Word2Vec(\n",
        "    sentences,\n",
        "    vector_size=10,\n",
        "    window=2,\n",
        "    min_count=1,\n",
        "    workers=1\n",
        ")\n",
        "print(\"\\nWords similar to 'java':\")\n",
        "print(\n",
        "    model.wv.most_similar(\n",
        "        'java',\n",
        "        topn=3\n",
        "    )\n",
        ")\n",
        "print(\"\\nWords similar to 'python':\")\n",
        "print(\n",
        "    model.wv.most_similar(\n",
        "        'python',\n",
        "        topn=4\n",
        "    )\n",
        ")\n",
        "print(\"\\nVocabulary:\")\n",
        "print(list(model.wv.index_to_key))\n",
        "print(\"\\nVector representation of 'python':\")\n",
        "print(model.wv['python'])\n",
        "print(\"\\nVector size:\")\n",
        "print(len(model.wv['python']))\n",
        "print(\"\\nWords similar to 'language':\")\n",
        "print(\n",
        "    model.wv.most_similar(\n",
        "        'language',\n",
        "        topn=3\n",
        "    )\n",
        ")\n",
        "print(\"\\nWords similar to 'easy':\")\n",
        "print(\n",
        "    model.wv.most_similar(\n",
        "        'easy',\n",
        "        topn=3\n",
        "    )\n",
        ")\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n",
        "print(\"ONE-HOT ENCODING vs WORD2VEC\")\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 60)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\"\"\n",
        "ONE-HOT ENCODING\n",
        "----------------\n",
        "- Each word gets a vector.\n",
        "- Vector contains mostly 0s and one 1.\n",
        "- Does not learn semantic meaning.\n",
        "- Similar words are not automatically represented as similar.\n",
        "- Vector size depends on vocabulary size.\n",
        "\n",
        "\n",
        "WORD2VEC\n",
        "--------\n",
        "- Learns word representations from surrounding words.\n",
        "- Each word gets a dense numerical vector.\n",
        "- Can capture relationships between words.\n",
        "- Similar words can have similar vectors.\n",
        "- Vector size is chosen using vector_size.\n",
        "- Uses surrounding/context words to learn representations.\n",
        "\"\"\")"
      ]

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "!pip install -q sentence-transformers scikit-learn matplotlib\n",
        "from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer\n",
        "from sklearn.metrics.pairwise import cosine_similarity\n",
        "from sklearn.decomposition import PCA\n",
        "import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n",
        "import pandas as pd\n",
        "model = SentenceTransformer(\"all-MiniLM-L6-v2\")\n",
        "print(\"Model loaded successfully!\")\n",
        "sentence = \"I love learning about Artificial Intelligence.\"\n",
        "embedding = model.encode(sentence)\n",
        "print(\"\\nSentence:\")\n",
        "print(sentence)\n",
        "print(\"\\nEmbedding shape:\")\n",
        "print(embedding.shape)\n",
        "print(\"\\nFirst 10 embedding values:\")\n",
        "print(embedding[:10])\n",
        "sentences = [\n",
        "    \"I love dogs\",\n",
        "    \"I like puppies\",\n",
        "    \"The stock market crashed today\",\n",
        "    \"Cats are cute pets\",\n",
        "    \"The economy is in recession\"\n",
        "]\n",
        "embeddings = model.encode(sentences)\n",
        "print(\"\\nNumber of sentences:\")\n",
        "print(len(sentences))\n",
        "print(\"\\nEmbedding matrix shape:\")\n",
        "print(embeddings.shape)\n",
        "similarity_matrix = cosine_similarity(embeddings)\n",
        "df = pd.DataFrame(\n",
        "    similarity_matrix,\n",
        "    index=sentences,\n",
        "    columns=sentences\n",
        ")\n",
        "print(\"\\nCosine Similarity Matrix:\")\n",
        "display(df.round(2))\n",
        "pca = PCA(n_components=2)\n",
        "reduced = pca.fit_transform(embeddings)\n",
        "plt.figure(figsize=(7, 5))\n",
        "plt.scatter(\n",
        "    reduced[:, 0],\n",
        "    reduced[:, 1]\n",
        ")\n",
        "for i, sent in enumerate(sentences):\n",
        "\n",
        "    plt.annotate(\n",
        "        sent,\n",
        "        (\n",
        "            reduced[i, 0],\n",
        "            reduced[i, 1]\n",
        "        ),\n",
        "        fontsize=9\n",
        "    )\n",
        "plt.title(\n",
        "    \"2D Visualization of Sentence Embeddings\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "plt.xlabel(\n",
        "    \"PCA Dimension 1\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "plt.ylabel(\n",
        "    \"PCA Dimension 2\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "plt.grid(True)\n",
        "plt.show()\n",
        "documents = [\n",
        "\n",
        "    \"Python is a popular programming language for AI.\",\n",
        "\n",
        "    \"The weather today is sunny and warm.\",\n",
        "\n",
        "    \"Machine learning models learn patterns from data.\",\n",
        "\n",
        "    \"I enjoy playing football on weekends.\",\n",
        "\n",
        "    \"Deep learning uses neural networks with many layers.\",\n",
        "\n",
        "    \"Paris is the capital city of France.\"\n",
        "]\n",
        "doc_embeddings = model.encode(documents)\n",
        "def semantic_search(query, top_k=2):\n",
        "    query_embedding = model.encode([query])\n",
        "    scores = cosine_similarity(\n",
        "        query_embedding,\n",
        "        doc_embeddings\n",
        "    )[0]\n",
        "    ranked_idx = scores.argsort()[::-1][:top_k]\n",
        "    print(f\"\\nQuery: {query}\\n\")\n",
        "    for idx in ranked_idx:\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(\n",
        "            f\"Score: {scores[idx]:.3f} | \"\n",
        "            f\"{documents[idx]}\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "semantic_search(\n",
        "    \"Tell me about AI and neural networks\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "semantic_search(\n",
        "    \"What is the capital of France?\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "my_documents = [\n",
        "\n",
        "    \"Python is widely used for machine learning.\",\n",
        "\n",
        "    \"Deep learning uses neural networks.\",\n",
        "\n",
        "    \"Java is a popular programming language.\",\n",
        "\n",
        "    \"Football is played by two teams.\",\n",
        "\n",
        "    \"Cricket is a popular sport in India.\",\n",
        "\n",
        "    \"The weather is rainy today.\",\n",
        "\n",
        "    \"Artificial intelligence allows machines to perform intelligent tasks.\",\n",
        "\n",
        "    \"Chennai is a major city in Tamil Nadu.\",\n",
        "\n",
        "    \"Vector databases store numerical representations of data.\",\n",
        "\n",
        "    \"RAG retrieves relevant information before generating an answer.\"\n",
        "]\n",
        "my_doc_embeddings = model.encode(\n",
        "    my_documents\n",
        ")\n",
        "def my_semantic_search(query, top_k=3):\n",
        "    query_embedding = model.encode([query])\n",
        "    scores = cosine_similarity(\n",
        "        query_embedding,\n",
        "        my_doc_embeddings\n",
        "    )[0]\n",
        "    ranked_idx = scores.argsort()[::-1][:top_k]\n",
        "    print(f\"\\nQuery: {query}\\n\")\n",
        "    for idx in ranked_idx:\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(\n",
        "            f\"Score: {scores[idx]:.3f} | \"\n",
        "            f\"{my_documents[idx]}\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "my_semantic_search(\n",
        "    \"How are neural networks used in AI?\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "my_semantic_search(\n",
        "    \"Tell me about sports\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "my_semantic_search(\n",
        "    \"How does RAG find information?\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "model_mpnet = SentenceTransformer(\n",
        "    \"all-mpnet-base-v2\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "comparison_sentences = [\n",
        "    \"I love dogs\",\n",
        "    \"I like puppies\",\n",
        "    \"The stock market crashed today\",\n",
        "    \"Cats are cute pets\",\n",
        "    \"The economy is in recession\"\n",
        "]\n",
        "minilm_embeddings = model.encode(\n",
        "    comparison_sentences\n",
        ")\n",
        "mpnet_embeddings = model_mpnet.encode(\n",
        "    comparison_sentences\n",
        ")\n",
        "minilm_similarity = cosine_similarity(\n",
        "    minilm_embeddings\n",
        ")\n",
        "mpnet_similarity = cosine_similarity(\n",
        "    mpnet_embeddings\n",
        ")\n",
        "minilm_df = pd.DataFrame(\n",
        "    minilm_similarity,\n",
        "    index=comparison_sentences,\n",
        "    columns=comparison_sentences\n",
        ")\n",
        "mpnet_df = pd.DataFrame(\n",
        "    mpnet_similarity,\n",
        "    index=comparison_sentences,\n",
        "    columns=comparison_sentences\n",
        ")\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 70)\n",
        "print(\"ALL-MINILM-L6-V2 SIMILARITY\")\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 70)\n",
        "display(minilm_df.round(3))\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 70)\n",
        "print(\"ALL-MPNET-BASE-V2 SIMILARITY\")\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 70)\n",
        "display(mpnet_df.round(3))\n",
        "minilm_score = minilm_similarity[0][1]\n",
        "mpnet_score = mpnet_similarity[0][1]\n",
        "print(\"\\nSimilarity between:\")\n",
        "print('\"I love dogs\"')\n",
        "print('and')\n",
        "print('\"I like puppies\"')\n",
        "print(\n",
        "    f\"\\nMiniLM score : {minilm_score:.4f}\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "print(\n",
        "    f\"MPNet score  : {mpnet_score:.4f}\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "faq_questions = [\n",
        "\n",
        "    \"What is artificial intelligence?\",\n",
        "\n",
        "    \"What is machine learning?\",\n",
        "\n",
        "    \"What is deep learning?\",\n",
        "\n",
        "    \"What is a vector database?\",\n",
        "\n",
        "    \"What is RAG?\"\n",
        "]\n",
        "faq_answers = [\n",
        "\n",
        "    \"Artificial Intelligence is the field of building systems that can perform tasks that normally require human intelligence.\",\n",
        "\n",
        "    \"Machine Learning is a branch of AI where systems learn patterns from data.\",\n",
        "\n",
        "    \"Deep Learning is a type of machine learning that uses neural networks with multiple layers.\",\n",
        "\n",
        "    \"A vector database stores numerical vector representations and allows similarity search.\",\n",
        "\n",
        "    \"RAG stands for Retrieval-Augmented Generation. It retrieves relevant information and provides it to an LLM to help generate an answer.\"\n",
        "]\n",
        "faq_embeddings = model.encode(\n",
        "    faq_questions\n",
        ")\n",
        "def faq_bot(user_question):\n",
        "    query_embedding = model.encode(\n",
        "        [user_question]\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    scores = cosine_similarity(\n",
        "        query_embedding,\n",
        "        faq_embeddings\n",
        "    )[0]\n",
        "    best_index = scores.argmax()\n",
        "    best_score = scores[best_index]\n",
        "    print(\n",
        "        \"\\nUser Question:\",\n",
        "        user_question\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    print(\n",
        "        \"\\nMatched FAQ:\",\n",
        "        faq_questions[best_index]\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    print(\n",
        "        f\"Similarity Score: {best_score:.3f}\"\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    print(\n",
        "        \"\\nAnswer:\",\n",
        "        faq_answers[best_index]\n",
        "    )\n",
        "faq_bot(\n",
        "    \"Can you explain artificial intelligence?\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "faq_bot(\n",
        "    \"How does machine learning work?\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "faq_bot(\n",
        "    \"What are neural networks and deep learning?\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "faq_bot(\n",
        "    \"Where can I store embeddings for similarity search?\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "faq_bot(\n",
        "    \"How does retrieval augmented generation work?\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "def safer_faq_bot(\n",
        "    user_question,\n",
        "    threshold=0.35\n",
        "):\n",
        "    query_embedding = model.encode(\n",
        "        [user_question]\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    scores = cosine_similarity(\n",
        "        query_embedding,\n",
        "        faq_embeddings\n",
        "    )[0]\n",
        "    best_index = scores.argmax()\n",
        "    best_score = scores[best_index]\n",
        "    print(\n",
        "        \"\\nUser Question:\",\n",
        "        user_question\n",
        "    )\n",
        "    if best_score >= threshold:\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(\n",
        "            \"\\nMatched FAQ:\",\n",
        "            faq_questions[best_index]\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(\n",
        "            f\"Similarity Score: {best_score:.3f}\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(\n",
        "            \"\\nAnswer:\",\n",
        "            faq_answers[best_index]\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "    else:\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(\n",
        "            \"\\nNo sufficiently similar FAQ question was found.\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "\n",
        "        print(\n",
        "            f\"Best similarity score: {best_score:.3f}\"\n",
        "        )\n",
        "safer_faq_bot(\n",
        "    \"Tell me about vector databases\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "safer_faq_bot(\n",
        "    \"What is the population of Australia?\"\n",
        ")\n",
        "print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 70)\n",
        "print(\"HUGGING FACE EMBEDDINGS - FINAL RECAP\")\n",
        "print(\"=\" * 70)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\"\"\n",
        "1. EMBEDDING\n",
        "   -> A numerical vector representing the meaning of text.\n",
        "\n",
        "2. HUGGING FACE MODEL\n",
        "   -> We used all-MiniLM-L6-v2.\n",
        "\n",
        "3. EMBEDDING DIMENSION\n",
        "   -> all-MiniLM-L6-v2 produces 384-dimensional vectors.\n",
        "\n",
        "4. COSINE SIMILARITY\n",
        "   -> Measures how close two embedding vectors are.\n",
        "\n",
        "5. PCA\n",
        "   -> Reduces high-dimensional embeddings to 2 dimensions\n",
        "      for visualization.\n",
        "\n",
        "6. SEMANTIC SEARCH\n",
        "   -> Searches based on meaning instead of exact keywords.\n",
        "\n",
        "7. FAQ BOT\n",
        "   -> Embeds questions and retrieves the most similar question.\n",
        "\n",
        "8. RAG CONNECTION\n",
        "   -> Documents can be converted into embeddings,\n",
        "      stored in a vector database, retrieved using\n",
        "      semantic similarity, and then supplied to an LLM.\n",
        "\"\"\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nPractice complete!\")"

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "vector_A = [2, 4, 1]\n",
        "vector_B = [1, 3, 5]\n",
        "print(\"Vector A:\", vector_A)\n",
        "print(\"Vector B:\", vector_B)\n",
        "def dot_product(A, B):\n",
        "    total = 0\n",
        "    for a, b in zip(A, B):\n",
        "        total += a * b\n",
        "    return total\n",
        "dp = dot_product(vector_A, vector_B)\n",
        "print(\"\\nDot Product (A . B):\", dp)\n",
        "import math\n",
        "def magnitude(A):\n",
        "    total = 0\n",
        "    for a in A:\n",
        "        total += a ** 2\n",
        "    return math.sqrt(total)\n",
        "mag_A = magnitude(vector_A)\n",
        "mag_B = magnitude(vector_B)\n",
        "print(\"Magnitude of A:\", round(mag_A, 4))\n",
        "print(\"Magnitude of B:\", round(mag_B, 4))\n",
        "def cosine_similarity_manual(A, B):\n",
        "    dp = dot_product(A, B)\n",
        "    mag_A = magnitude(A)\n",
        "    mag_B = magnitude(B)\n",
        "    similarity = dp / (mag_A * mag_B)\n",
        "    return similarity\n",
        "result_manual = cosine_similarity_manual(vector_A, vector_B)\n",
        "print(\n",
        "    \"Cosine Similarity (Manual Calculation):\",\n",
        "    round(result_manual, 4)\n",
        ")\n",
        "import numpy as np\n",
        "A = np.array(vector_A)\n",
        "B = np.array(vector_B)\n",
        "dot = np.dot(A, B)\n",
        "norm_A = np.linalg.norm(A)\n",
        "norm_B = np.linalg.norm(B)\n",
        "result_numpy = dot / (norm_A * norm_B)\n",
        "print(\n",
        "    \"\\nCosine Similarity (NumPy):\",\n",
        "    round(result_numpy, 4)\n",
        ")\n",
        "!pip install -q scikit-learn\n",
        "from sklearn.metrics.pairwise import cosine_similarity\n",
        "A_2d = np.array(vector_A).reshape(1, -1)\n",
        "B_2d = np.array(vector_B).reshape(1, -1)\n",
        "result_sklearn = cosine_similarity(A_2d, B_2d)[0][0]\n",
        "print(\n",
        "    \"Cosine Similarity (scikit-learn):\",\n",
        "    round(result_sklearn, 4)\n",
        ")\n",
        "print(\"\\nManual      :\", round(result_manual, 6))\n",
        "print(\"NumPy       :\", round(result_numpy, 6))\n",
        "print(\"scikit-learn:\", round(result_sklearn, 6))\n",
        "assert (\n",
        "    round(result_manual, 4)\n",
        "    == round(result_numpy, 4)\n",
        "    == round(result_sklearn, 4)\n",
        ")\n",
        "print(\"\\nAll three methods match!\")\n",
        "examples = {\n",
        "    \"Identical vectors\":\n",
        "        ([1, 2, 3], [1, 2, 3]),\n",
        "    \"Opposite vectors\":\n",
        "        ([1, 2, 3], [-1, -2, -3]),\n",
        "    \"Perpendicular vectors\":\n",
        "        ([1, 0], [0, 1]),\n",
        "    \"Scaled vectors (same direction)\":\n",
        "        ([1, 2, 3], [2, 4, 6]),\n",
        "}\n",
        "for name, (a, b) in examples.items():\n",
        "    sim = cosine_similarity_manual(a, b)\n",
        "    print(f\"{name:35s} -> {round(sim, 4)}\")\n",
        "\n",
        "def dot_product_comprehension(A, B):\n",
        "    return sum(a * b for a, b in zip(A, B))\n",
        "\n",
        "def magnitude_comprehension(A):\n",
        "    return math.sqrt(sum(a ** 2 for a in A))\n",
        "\n",
        "def cosine_similarity_comprehension(A, B):\n",
        "    dp = dot_product_comprehension(A, B)\n",
        "    mag_A = magnitude_comprehension(A)\n",
        "    mag_B = magnitude_comprehension(B)\n",
        "    return dp / (mag_A * mag_B)\n",
        "exercise_1_result = cosine_similarity_comprehension(\n",
        "    vector_A,\n",
        "    vector_B\n",
        ")\n",
        "print(\n",
        "    \"\\nPractice 1 - List Comprehension Result:\",\n",
        "    round(exercise_1_result, 4)\n",
        ")\n",
        "exercise_2_A = [1, 1, 1]\n",
        "exercise_2_B = [5, 5, 5]\n",
        "exercise_2_result = cosine_similarity_manual(\n",
        "    exercise_2_A,\n",
        "    exercise_2_B\n",
        ")\n",
        "print(\n",
        "    \"\\nPractice 2 - [1,1,1] vs [5,5,5]:\",\n",
        "    round(exercise_2_result, 4)\n",
        ")\n",
        "assert round(exercise_2_result, 4) == 1.0\n",
        "print(\"Prediction was correct: similarity = 1\")\n",
        "\n",
        "!pip install -q sentence-transformers\n",
        "from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer\n",
        "hf_model = SentenceTransformer(\"all-MiniLM-L6-v2\")\n",
        "sentence_A = \"I love dogs\"\n",
        "sentence_B = \"I like puppies\"\n",
        "embedding_A = hf_model.encode(sentence_A)\n",

        "    embedding_similarity_manual - embedding_similarity_sklearn\n",

      "cell_type": "code",
      "source": [
        "!pip install -q langchain langchain-text-splitters tiktoken pypdf fpdf2 pandas matplotlib\n",
        "\n",
        "import os\n",
        "import re\n",
        "import tiktoken\n",
        "import pandas as pd\n",
        "import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n",
        "from fpdf import FPDF\n",
        "from pypdf import PdfReader\n",
        "from langchain_text_splitters import CharacterTextSplitter, RecursiveCharacterTextSplitter\n",
        "\n",
        "os.makedirs(\"docs\", exist_ok=True)\n",
        "\n",
        "article_text = \"\"\"Artificial Intelligence: An Overview\n",
        "\n",
        "Artificial Intelligence, or AI, is the field of computer science focused on building systems\n",
        "that can perform tasks which normally require human intelligence. These tasks include\n",
        "understanding language, recognizing images, making decisions, and learning from experience.\n",
        "\n",
        "Machine Learning is a subset of AI where systems learn patterns from data instead of following\n",
        "hardcoded rules. Instead of programming every decision by hand, we feed the system many examples,\n",
        "and it learns the underlying pattern on its own. This approach powers everything from spam filters\n",
        "to recommendation systems.\n",
        "\n",
        "Deep Learning takes Machine Learning further by using neural networks with many layers. These\n",
        "layered networks can automatically discover complex patterns in large amounts of data, such as\n",
        "recognizing faces in photos or understanding the meaning of a sentence.\n",
        "\n",
        "Natural Language Processing, or NLP, is the branch of AI that focuses specifically on human\n",
        "language. NLP powers chatbots, translation tools, and search engines. A key building block of\n",
        "modern NLP is the embedding, which converts words or sentences into numeric vectors that capture\n",
        "meaning.\n",
        "\n",
        "Retrieval-Augmented Generation, or RAG, is a technique that combines a search system with a\n",
        "language model. Instead of relying only on what a language model memorized during training, RAG\n",
        "first retrieves relevant, up-to-date information from a document collection, then passes that\n",
        "information to the model so it can generate a more accurate, grounded answer.\n",
        "\n",
        "Vector Databases store embeddings and allow fast similarity search across millions of documents.\n",
        "They are a core infrastructure piece behind modern RAG systems, chatbots, and recommendation\n",
        "engines used by companies around the world today.\n",
        "\"\"\"\n",
        "\n",
        "with open(\"docs/sample_article.txt\", \"w\") as f:\n",
        "    f.write(article_text)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"Document created: docs/sample_article.txt\")\n",
        "print(\"Total characters:\", len(article_text))\n",
        "print(\"Total words:\", len(article_text.split()))\n",
        "\n",
        "with open(\"docs/sample_article.txt\", \"r\") as f:\n",
        "    loaded_text = f.read()\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nLoaded document preview:\\n\")\n",
        "print(loaded_text[:300], \"...\")\n",
        "\n",
        "pdf = FPDF()\n",
        "pdf.add_page()\n",
        "pdf.set_font(\"Times\", size=12)\n",
        "\n",
        "for line in article_text.split(\"\\n\"):\n",
        "    pdf.multi_cell(190, 8, line)\n",
        "\n",
        "pdf.output(\"docs/sample_article.pdf\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nPDF created: docs/sample_article.pdf\")\n",
        "\n",
        "reader = PdfReader(\"docs/sample_article.pdf\")\n",
        "\n",
        "pdf_text = \"\"\n",
        "for page in reader.pages:\n",
        "    pdf_text += page.extract_text() + \"\\n\"\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"Pages found:\", len(reader.pages))\n",
        "print(\"\\nExtracted PDF text preview:\\n\")\n",
        "print(pdf_text[:300], \"...\")\n",
        "\n",
        "def fixed_size_chunking(text, chunk_size=300, overlap=50):\n",
        "    chunks = []\n",
        "    start = 0\n",
        "    while start < len(text):\n",
        "        end = start + chunk_size\n",
        "        chunks.append(text[start:end])\n",
        "        start += chunk_size - overlap\n",
        "    return chunks\n",
        "\n",
        "fixed_chunks = fixed_size_chunking(\n",
        "    loaded_text,\n",
        "    chunk_size=300,\n",
        "    overlap=50\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nFixed-size chunking\")\n",
        "print(\"Number of chunks:\", len(fixed_chunks))\n",
        "\n",
        "for i, chunk in enumerate(fixed_chunks):\n",
        "    print(f\"\\n--- Chunk {i+1} ({len(chunk)} chars) ---\")\n",
        "    print(chunk)\n",
        "\n",
        "char_splitter = CharacterTextSplitter(\n",
        "    separator=\"\\n\\n\",\n",
        "    chunk_size=300,\n",
        "    chunk_overlap=50\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "char_chunks = char_splitter.split_text(loaded_text)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nCharacterTextSplitter\")\n",
        "print(\"Number of chunks:\", len(char_chunks))\n",
        "\n",
        "for i, chunk in enumerate(char_chunks):\n",
        "    print(f\"\\n--- Chunk {i+1} ({len(chunk)} chars) ---\")\n",
        "    print(chunk)\n",
        "\n",
        "recursive_splitter = RecursiveCharacterTextSplitter(\n",
        "    chunk_size=300,\n",
        "    chunk_overlap=50,\n",
        "    separators=[\"\\n\\n\", \"\\n\", \". \", \" \", \"\"]\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "recursive_chunks = recursive_splitter.split_text(loaded_text)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nRecursiveCharacterTextSplitter\")\n",
        "print(\"Number of chunks:\", len(recursive_chunks))\n",
        "\n",
        "for i, chunk in enumerate(recursive_chunks):\n",
        "    print(f\"\\n--- Chunk {i+1} ({len(chunk)} chars) ---\")\n",
        "    print(chunk)\n",
        "\n",
        "def sentence_chunking(text, sentences_per_chunk=2):\n",
        "    sentences = re.split(r'(?<=[.!?])\\s+', text.strip())\n",
        "    sentences = [s for s in sentences if s]\n",
        "\n",
        "    chunks = []\n",
        "\n",
        "    for i in range(0, len(sentences), sentences_per_chunk):\n",
        "        chunk = \" \".join(sentences[i:i + sentences_per_chunk])\n",
        "        chunks.append(chunk)\n",
        "\n",
        "    return chunks\n",
        "\n",
        "sentence_chunks = sentence_chunking(\n",
        "    loaded_text,\n",
        "    sentences_per_chunk=2\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nSentence-based chunking\")\n",
        "print(\"Number of chunks:\", len(sentence_chunks))\n",
        "\n",
        "for i, chunk in enumerate(sentence_chunks):\n",
        "    print(f\"\\n--- Chunk {i+1} ---\")\n",
        "    print(chunk)\n",
        "\n",
        "encoding = tiktoken.get_encoding(\"cl100k_base\")\n",
        "\n",
        "def token_chunking(text, max_tokens=60, overlap_tokens=10):\n",
        "    tokens = encoding.encode(text)\n",
        "    chunks = []\n",
        "    start = 0\n",
        "\n",
        "    while start < len(tokens):\n",
        "        end = start + max_tokens\n",
        "        chunk_tokens = tokens[start:end]\n",
        "        chunks.append(encoding.decode(chunk_tokens))\n",
        "        start += max_tokens - overlap_tokens\n",
        "\n",
        "    return chunks\n",
        "\n",
        "token_chunks = token_chunking(\n",
        "    loaded_text,\n",
        "    max_tokens=60,\n",
        "    overlap_tokens=10\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nToken-based chunking\")\n",
        "print(\"Number of chunks:\", len(token_chunks))\n",
        "\n",
        "for i, chunk in enumerate(token_chunks):\n",
        "    token_count = len(encoding.encode(chunk))\n",
        "    print(f\"\\n--- Chunk {i+1} ({token_count} tokens) ---\")\n",
        "    print(chunk)\n",
        "\n",
        "comparison = pd.DataFrame({\n",
        "    \"Strategy\": [\n",
        "        \"Fixed-size (scratch)\",\n",
        "        \"CharacterTextSplitter\",\n",
        "        \"RecursiveCharacterTextSplitter\",\n",
        "        \"Sentence-based\",\n",
        "        \"Token-based\"\n",
        "    ],\n",
        "    \"Number of Chunks\": [\n",
        "        len(fixed_chunks),\n",
        "        len(char_chunks),\n",
        "        len(recursive_chunks),\n",
        "        len(sentence_chunks),\n",
        "        len(token_chunks)\n",
        "    ],\n",
        "    \"Avg Chunk Length (chars)\": [\n",
        "        round(sum(len(c) for c in fixed_chunks) / len(fixed_chunks), 1),\n",
        "        round(sum(len(c) for c in char_chunks) / len(char_chunks), 1),\n",
        "        round(sum(len(c) for c in recursive_chunks) / len(recursive_chunks), 1),\n",
        "        round(sum(len(c) for c in sentence_chunks) / len(sentence_chunks), 1),\n",
        "        round(sum(len(c) for c in token_chunks) / len(token_chunks), 1)\n",
        "    ]\n",
        "})\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nComparison\")\n",
        "display(comparison)\n",
        "\n",
        "plt.figure(figsize=(8, 5))\n",
        "plt.bar(comparison[\"Strategy\"], comparison[\"Number of Chunks\"])\n",
        "plt.xticks(rotation=30, ha=\"right\")\n",
        "plt.ylabel(\"Number of Chunks\")\n",
        "plt.title(\"Number of Chunks Produced by Each Strategy\")\n",
        "plt.tight_layout()\n",
        "plt.show()\n",
        "\n",
        "recursive_splitter_150 = RecursiveCharacterTextSplitter(\n",
        "    chunk_size=150,\n",
        "    chunk_overlap=50,\n",
        "    separators=[\"\\n\\n\", \"\\n\", \". \", \" \", \"\"]\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "recursive_chunks_150 = recursive_splitter_150.split_text(loaded_text)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nExercise 1: RecursiveCharacterTextSplitter with chunk_size=150\")\n",
        "print(\"Number of chunks:\", len(recursive_chunks_150))\n",
        "\n",
        "for i, chunk in enumerate(recursive_chunks_150):\n",
        "    print(f\"\\n--- Chunk {i+1} ({len(chunk)} chars) ---\")\n",
        "    print(chunk)\n",
        "\n",
        "sentence_chunks_1 = sentence_chunking(\n",
        "    loaded_text,\n",
        "    sentences_per_chunk=1\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "sentence_chunks_4 = sentence_chunking(\n",
        "    loaded_text,\n",
        "    sentences_per_chunk=4\n",
        ")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nExercise 2: Sentence-based chunking\")\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nsentences_per_chunk=1\")\n",
        "print(\"Number of chunks:\", len(sentence_chunks_1))\n",
        "\n",
        "for i, chunk in enumerate(sentence_chunks_1):\n",
        "    print(f\"\\n--- Chunk {i+1} ---\")\n",
        "    print(chunk)\n",
        "\n",
        "print(\"\\nsentences_per_chunk=4\")\n",
        "print(\"Number of chunks:\", len(sentence_chunks_4))\n",
        "\n",
        "for i, chunk in enumerate(sentence_chunks_4):\n",
        "    print(f\"\\n--- Chunk {i+1} ---\")\n",
        "    print(chunk)\n",
        "\n",
        "exercise_2_comparison = pd.DataFrame({\n",
        "    \"Setting\": [\n",
        "        \"sentences_per_chunk=1\",\n",
        "        \"sentences_per_chunk=4\"\n",
        "    ],\n",
        "    \"Number of Chunks\": [\n",
        "        len(sentence_chunks_1),\n",
        "        len(sentence_chunks_4)\n",

        "        round(sum(len(c) for c in sentence_chunks_1) / len(sentence_chunks_1), 1),\n",

              "        const dataTable =\n",

              "          + ' to learn more about interactive tables.';\n",
